# Current all-claim later-scenario review supplement

Prepared **5 October 2026** for the task begun 4 October. The `2026-10-04` filenames identify that task; they do not backdate this content. This is a prospective companion to the preserved 3 October application and PDF, containing **199 exact candidate texts and 796 explicit preliminary scenario records**. The 99 numbered application paragraphs, all current/historical claims and source captures remain unchanged.

[Machine-readable complete assessments](../data/claim_scenarios_2026-10-04.json) bind the current specification, map, claim review and selected source comparisons by SHA-256. Every scenario carries its own feature, complete parent combination, disclosed/missing features, bounded calculation/source context, possible section 102/103 arguments and unresolved questions. Shared family reasoning does not import sibling limitations.

No filing, complete chronological search, final patentability or inventorship determination, universal physical enablement or universal defensive exclusion is asserted. Robert M. Layne remains the user-directed proposed sole inventor. Applicant physical experiments remain unperformed and not planned. This sidecar is not silently incorporated into an earlier application or public version.

## Comparison types

| Type | Representative comparison | Boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S1 | Selected source-type or bounded record arrangement | A present mathematical witness is not an earlier enabled physical embodiment. An attributed source retains its actual specimen, basis, process and observation boundaries. |
| S2 | Same elements with changed ratios, structure or state; qualified record-operation variation | Physical comparisons preserve the selected elemental support and k. A changed ratio/state can fail an exact added restriction while retaining those labels. Independent method comparisons preserve their own categorical and dependency boundaries; corruption/rejection probes are separate notes. |
| S3 | Specific selection from the broad catalog | An enabled disclosed species can be compared with a broader genus; reverse membership does not automatically disclose every selected species. Record workflows need their complete ordered operations. |
| S4 | Added preparation, use, control or recording acts | Product identity, target arithmetic and record fields do not supply every later process/use. Intended-use words and operative method steps require different construction. |

The exact candidate text and full dependency chain control each comparison. These are representative hypothetical later arrangements, not allegations about identified patents. A numerical witness records arithmetic only; a source report supplies its bounded attributed teaching. A later physical or method claim needs all its actual additional limitations and qualified chronology.

## Bounded examples and source use

CALCULATED contexts cover exact-k targets, binary normalization, the original grid and unrestricted rationals, correlated intervals, joint isotope counts, molecular/chain/phase accounting, volume and mass bounds, finite run prefixes and rejected records. Actual structure, state, preparation and outcomes remain unsupported where the source does not supply them. Anonymous model inputs are explicitly stipulated; their values are not measurements.

The retained source profiles are alloy, metallic glass, oxide, molecular, polymer, hydrogel, electrical/composite, nuclear and particle records. Their complete retained support, source operating gaps, source editions and source-local conflicts are carried from the 3 October claim review. The supplement's source identifiers refer to that reviewed official-source registry; a separately dated procedural/counsel review governs any current-law update.

## Individual candidate crosswalk

### Claim 1

A material comprising a closed elemental inventory of k distinct elemental constituents selected from atomic numbers 1 through 138, wherein k is an integer from 2 through 138, each selected constituent has a positive normalized fraction x_i on an expressly identified atomic-fraction or mass-fraction basis, the fractions of the selected constituents sum to one, and constituents outside the selected inventory have zero fraction on that basis.

Full inherited claims: **none (independent)**. Family **G01**. Operative combination paragraph locators: [0010], [0011], [0014], [0015], [0016], [0080]. Current support/assumption/gap assessment: claim 1 of the [preserved current review](../data/claim_review_2026-10-03.json).

The simplex defines targets but supplies no general preparation/use route for arbitrary recognized/hypothetical mixtures. ## Verification boundary

Run `python tools/claim_scenario_checks.py` from the repository root. The dependency-free check independently parses the operative claim annex, resolves actual direct/transitive dependencies, verifies exact texts/features and source hashes, checks four explicit records per claim, rejects missing own-feature or sibling combinations and checks evidence/status boundaries. Its self-test introduces failures only in temporary in-memory copies and requires rejection. Arithmetic checks cover selected independent examples; they do not prove physical preparation or legal results.
